  727 |             out_channels,
  728 |             time_dim,
  729 |             groups=groups,
  730 |             dropout=dropout,
  731 |         )
  732 | 
  733 |     def forward(self, x, skip, time_emb):
  734 |         x = self.up(x)
  735 |         if x.shape[-1] != skip.shape[-1]:
  736 |             x = F.interpolate(x, size=skip.shape[-1], mode="linear", align_corners=False)
  737 |         return self.block(torch.cat([x, skip], dim=1), time_emb)
SOURCE src/models/mambattention.py:753 — UNetDilatedConvMidBlock1d
  753 | class UNetDilatedConvMidBlock1d(nn.Module):
  754 |     def __init__(self, channels, groups=8, dropout=0.0, dilations=(1, 2, 4, 8)):
  755 |         super().__init__()
  756 |         group_count = min(int(groups), int(channels))
  757 |         while channels % group_count != 0:
  758 |             group_count -= 1
  759 |         layers = []
  760 |         for dilation in dilations:
  761 |             dilation = int(dilation)
  762 |             layers.extend(
  763 |                 [
  764 |                     nn.Conv1d(channels, channels, 3, padding=dilation, dilation=dilation),
  765 |                     nn.GroupNorm(group_count, channels),
  766 |                     nn.SiLU(),
  767 |                     nn.Dropout(dropout),
  768 |                 ]
  769 |             )
  770 |         layers.append(nn.Conv1d(channels, channels, 1))
  771 |         self.net = nn.Sequential(*layers)
  772 | 
  773 |     def forward(self, x):
  774 |         return x + self.net(x)
SOURCE src/models/mambattention.py:777 — ConditionalResidualFlowUNetRefiner1d
  777 | class ConditionalResidualFlowUNetRefiner1d(nn.Module):
  778 |     def __init__(
  779 |         self,
  780 |         condition_channels=6,
  781 |         state_channels=2,
  782 |         output_channels=2,
  783 |         base_channels=64,
  784 |         channel_mults=(1, 2, 4),
  785 |         time_dim=192,
  786 |         groups=8,
  787 |         dropout=0.0,
  788 |         pool_scales=(3, 5, 9, 15),
  789 |         attention_heads=4,
  790 |         use_attention=True,
  791 |         mid_dilations=(1, 2, 4, 8),
  792 |         aux_output_channels=0,
  793 |     ):
  794 |         super().__init__()
  795 |         self.output_channels = int(output_channels)
  796 |         self.aux_output_channels = int(aux_output_channels)
  797 |         self.time_embedding = nn.Sequential(
  798 |             ResidualFlowTimeEmbedding(time_dim),
  799 |             nn.Linear(time_dim, time_dim),
  800 |             nn.SiLU(),
  801 |             nn.Linear(time_dim, time_dim),
  802 |         )
  803 |         channels = [int(base_channels) * int(mult) for mult in channel_mults]
  804 |         self.input = nn.Conv1d(condition_channels + state_channels, channels[0], 3, padding=1)
  805 |         self.downs = nn.ModuleList()
  806 |         in_channels = channels[0]
  807 |         for out_channels in channels:
  808 |             self.downs.append(
  809 |                 UNetDownBlock1d(
  810 |                     in_channels,
  811 |                     out_channels,
  812 |                     time_dim,
  813 |                     groups=groups,
  814 |                     dropout=dropout,
  815 |                 )
  816 |             )
  817 |             in_channels = out_channels
  818 |         self.first_skip_dapp = UNetDAPP1d(channels[0], pool_scales=pool_scales, use_global_pool=True)
  819 |         self.mid_block1 = UNetConvBlock1d(channels[-1], channels[-1], time_dim, groups=groups, dropout=dropout)
  820 |         if use_attention:
  821 |             self.mid_attention = UNetSelfAttention1d(channels[-1], heads=attention_heads)
  822 |         else:
  823 |             self.mid_attention = UNetDilatedConvMidBlock1d(
  824 |                 channels[-1],
  825 |                 groups=groups,
  826 |                 dropout=dropout,
  827 |                 dilations=mid_dilations,
  828 |             )
  829 |         self.mid_block2 = UNetConvBlock1d(channels[-1], channels[-1], time_dim, groups=groups, dropout=dropout)
  830 |         self.ups = nn.ModuleList()
  831 |         current = channels[-1]
  832 |         for skip_channels in reversed(channels):
  833 |             self.ups.append(
  834 |                 UNetUpBlock1d(
  835 |                     current,
  836 |                     skip_channels,
  837 |                     skip_channels,
  838 |                     time_dim,
  839 |                     groups=groups,
  840 |                     dropout=dropout,
  841 |                 )
  842 |             )
  843 |             current = skip_channels
  844 |         self.output = nn.Sequential(
  845 |             nn.GroupNorm(1, channels[0]),
  846 |             nn.SiLU(),
  847 |             nn.Conv1d(channels[0], self.output_channels + self.aux_output_channels, 3, padding=1),
  848 |         )
  849 | 
  850 |     def forward(self, residual_state, condition, t):
  851 |         if t.ndim == 0:
  852 |             t = t.expand(residual_state.shape[0])
  853 |         time_emb = self.time_embedding(t)
  854 |         x = self.input(torch.cat([residual_state, condition], dim=1))
  855 |         skips = []
  856 |         for index, down in enumerate(self.downs):
  857 |             x, skip = down(x, time_emb)
  858 |             if index == 0:
  859 |                 skip = self.first_skip_dapp(skip)
  860 |             skips.append(skip)
  861 |         x = self.mid_block1(x, time_emb)
  862 |         x = self.mid_attention(x)
  863 |         x = self.mid_block2(x, time_emb)
  864 |         for up, skip in zip(self.ups, reversed(skips)):
  865 |             x = up(x, skip, time_emb)
  866 |         return self.output(x)
SOURCE src/models/mambattention.py:869 — ResidualFlowDualPathDAPPMambAttentionCore
  869 | class ResidualFlowDualPathDAPPMambAttentionCore(DualPathDAPPMambAttentionCore):
  870 |     def __init__(self, config, block_cls=MambAttentionBlock):
  871 |         super().__init__(config, block_cls=block_cls)
  872 |         h = self.h
  873 |         self.cfm_inference_steps = int(h.get("cfm_inference_steps", 2))
  874 |         self.cfm_inference_shots = max(int(h.get("cfm_inference_shots", 1)), 1)
  875 |         self.cfm_inference_shot_aggregation = str(h.get("cfm_inference_shot_aggregation", "mean")).lower()
  876 |         if self.cfm_inference_shot_aggregation not in {"mean", "median"}:
  877 |             raise ValueError(
  878 |                 "model.cfm_inference_shot_aggregation must be one of: mean, median; "
  879 |                 f"got {self.cfm_inference_shot_aggregation!r}."
  880 |             )
  881 |         self.cfm_inference_shot_sampling = str(h.get("cfm_inference_shot_sampling", "independent")).lower()
  882 |         if self.cfm_inference_shot_sampling not in {"independent", "antithetic"}:
  883 |             raise ValueError(
  884 |                 "model.cfm_inference_shot_sampling must be one of: independent, antithetic; "
  885 |                 f"got {self.cfm_inference_shot_sampling!r}."
  886 |             )
  887 |         if self.cfm_inference_shot_sampling == "antithetic" and self.cfm_inference_shots % 2 != 0:
  888 |             raise ValueError("Antithetic inference shot sampling requires an even model.cfm_inference_shots value.")
  889 |         self.cfm_inference_start_noise_scale = float(h.get("cfm_inference_start_noise_scale", 0.0))
  890 |         self.cfm_train_noise_scale = float(h.get("cfm_train_noise_scale", 0.05))
  891 |         self.cfm_zero_start_prob = float(h.get("cfm_zero_start_prob", 0.5))
  892 |         self.cfm_bridge_noise_scale = float(h.get("cfm_bridge_noise_scale", 0.02))
  893 |         self.cfm_condition_channels = 6
  894 |         self.cfm_use_adaptive_gate = bool(h.get("cfm_use_adaptive_gate", True))
  895 |         self.cfm_clean_delta_budget = float(h.get("cfm_clean_delta_budget", 0.6))
  896 |         self.cfm_baseline_delta_budget = float(h.get("cfm_baseline_delta_budget", 0.8))
  897 |         self.cfm_project_baseline_delta = bool(h.get("cfm_project_baseline_delta", True))
  898 |         self.cfm_clean_lowpass_keep = float(h.get("cfm_clean_lowpass_keep", 0.25))
  899 |         self.cfm_refine_gate_max = float(h.get("cfm_refine_gate_max", 0.6))
  900 |         self.cfm_baseline_gate_max = float(h.get("cfm_baseline_gate_max", 0.6))
  901 |         self.cfm_consistency_blend_max = float(h.get("cfm_consistency_blend_max", 0.5))
  902 |         self.lambda_cfm = float(h.get("lambda_cfm", 0.2))
  903 |         self.lambda_cfm_baseline = float(h.get("lambda_cfm_baseline", self.lambda_cfm))
  904 |         self.lambda_cfm_recon = float(h.get("lambda_cfm_recon", 0.25))
  905 |         self.lambda_cfm_stft = float(h.get("lambda_cfm_stft", 0.05))
  906 |         self.lambda_cfm_lf = float(h.get("lambda_cfm_lf", 0.03))
  907 |         self.lambda_cfm_deriv = float(h.get("lambda_cfm_deriv", 0.03))
  908 |         self.lambda_cfm_residual_smooth = float(h.get("lambda_cfm_residual_smooth", 0.005))
  909 |         self.lambda_cfm_clean_baseline_consistency = float(h.get("lambda_cfm_clean_baseline_consistency", 0.05))
  910 |         gate_init = float(h.get("cfm_refine_gate_init", 0.15))
  911 |         gate_ratio = min(max(gate_init / max(self.cfm_refine_gate_max, 1.0e-6), 1.0e-6), 1.0 - 1.0e-6)
  912 |         self.cfm_refine_gate_raw = nn.Parameter(
  913 |             torch.tensor(math.log(gate_ratio / (1.0 - gate_ratio)), dtype=torch.float32)
  914 |         )
  915 |         baseline_gate_init = float(h.get("cfm_baseline_gate_init", gate_init))
  916 |         baseline_gate_ratio = min(max(baseline_gate_init / max(self.cfm_baseline_gate_max, 1.0e-6), 1.0e-6), 1.0 - 1.0e-6)
  917 |         self.cfm_baseline_gate_raw = nn.Parameter(
  918 |             torch.tensor(math.log(baseline_gate_ratio / (1.0 - baseline_gate_ratio)), dtype=torch.float32)
  919 |         )
  920 |         blend_init = float(h.get("cfm_consistency_blend_init", 0.25))
  921 |         blend_ratio = min(max(blend_init / max(self.cfm_consistency_blend_max, 1.0e-6), 1.0e-6), 1.0 - 1.0e-6)
  922 |         self.cfm_consistency_blend_raw = nn.Parameter(
  923 |             torch.tensor(math.log(blend_ratio / (1.0 - blend_ratio)), dtype=torch.float32)
  924 |         )
  925 |         if self.cfm_use_adaptive_gate:
  926 |             self.cfm_adaptive_gate = CFMAdaptiveGate(
  927 |                 condition_channels=self.cfm_condition_channels,
  928 |                 hidden=int(h.get("cfm_gate_hidden", 32)),
  929 |                 refine_gate_init=gate_init,
  930 |                 refine_gate_max=self.cfm_refine_gate_max,
  931 |                 baseline_gate_init=baseline_gate_init,
  932 |                 baseline_gate_max=self.cfm_baseline_gate_max,
  933 |                 blend_init=blend_init,
  934 |                 blend_max=self.cfm_consistency_blend_max,
  935 |             )
  936 |         else:
  937 |             self.cfm_adaptive_gate = None
  938 |         self.residual_flow = ConditionalResidualFlowRefiner1d(
  939 |             condition_channels=self.cfm_condition_channels,
  940 |             state_channels=2,
  941 |             output_channels=2,
  942 |             channels=int(h.get("cfm_channels", 48)),
  943 |             blocks=int(h.get("cfm_blocks", 4)),
  944 |             time_dim=int(h.get("cfm_time_dim", 96)),
  945 |             groups=int(h.get("cfm_groups", 8)),
  946 |             dropout=float(h.get("cfm_dropout", 0.0)),
  947 |             dilations=tuple(h.get("cfm_dilations", [1, 2, 4, 8])),
  948 |         )
  949 | 
  950 |     def _cfm_refine_gate(self):
  951 |         return self.cfm_refine_gate_max * torch.sigmoid(self.cfm_refine_gate_raw)
  952 | 
  953 |     def _cfm_baseline_gate(self):
  954 |         return self.cfm_baseline_gate_max * torch.sigmoid(self.cfm_baseline_gate_raw)
  955 | 
  956 |     def _cfm_consistency_blend(self):
  957 |         return self.cfm_consistency_blend_max * torch.sigmoid(self.cfm_consistency_blend_raw)
  958 | 
  959 |     def _cfm_gates(self, condition):
  960 |         if self.cfm_adaptive_gate is not None:
  961 |             return self.cfm_adaptive_gate(condition)
  962 |         batch = condition.shape[0]
  963 |         clean_gate = self._cfm_refine_gate().view(1, 1, 1).expand(batch, 1, 1)
  964 |         baseline_gate = self._cfm_baseline_gate().view(1, 1, 1).expand(batch, 1, 1)
  965 |         blend = self._cfm_consistency_blend().view(1, 1, 1).expand(batch, 1, 1)
  966 |         return clean_gate, baseline_gate, blend
  967 | 
  968 |     def _limit_delta(self, delta, reference, budget_ratio):
  969 |         if budget_ratio <= 0:
  970 |             return delta
  971 |         reference_budget = (
  972 |             reference.abs().mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
  973 |             + 0.25 * reference.std(dim=-1, keepdim=True)
  974 |         ).clamp_min(1.0e-6)
  975 |         delta_level = delta.abs().mean(dim=-1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1.0e-6)
  976 |         scale = (float(budget_ratio) * reference_budget / delta_level).clamp(max=1.0)
  977 |         return delta * scale
  978 | 
  979 |     def _flow_condition(self, noisy_audio, base_restored, baseline_hat=None):
  980 |         if noisy_audio.ndim == 2:
  981 |             noisy_audio = noisy_audio.unsqueeze(1)
  982 |         if base_restored.ndim == 2:
  983 |             base_restored = base_restored.unsqueeze(1)
  984 |         estimated_baseline = noisy_audio - base_restored
  985 |         if baseline_hat is None:
  986 |             baseline_hat = estimated_baseline
  987 |         low_noisy = self._baseline_projection(noisy_audio)
  988 |         high_base = base_restored - self._baseline_projection(base_restored)
  989 |         return torch.cat([noisy_audio, base_restored, estimated_baseline, baseline_hat, low_noisy, high_base], dim=1)
  990 | 
  991 |     def _sample_flow_start(self, target_residual):
  992 |         start = self.cfm_train_noise_scale * torch.randn_like(target_residual)
  993 |         if self.cfm_zero_start_prob > 0:
  994 |             zero_mask = (
  995 |                 torch.rand((target_residual.shape[0], 1, 1), device=target_residual.device)
  996 |                 < self.cfm_zero_start_prob
  997 |             )
  998 |             start = torch.where(zero_mask, torch.zeros_like(start), start)
  999 |         return start
 1000 | 
 1001 |     def _flow_matching_loss(self, condition, target_residual, channel_weight=1.0, valid_mask=None):
 1002 |         start = self._sample_flow_start(target_residual)
 1003 |         t = torch.rand((target_residual.shape[0],), device=target_residual.device)
 1004 |         t_view = t.view(-1, 1, 1)
 1005 |         bridge_noise = self.cfm_bridge_noise_scale * torch.sin(torch.pi * t_view) * torch.randn_like(target_residual)
 1006 |         residual_state = (1.0 - t_view) * start + t_view * target_residual + bridge_noise
 1007 |         target_velocity = target_residual - start
 1008 |         predicted_velocity = self.residual_flow(residual_state, condition, t)
 1009 |         loss = F.mse_loss(predicted_velocity, target_velocity, reduction="none")
 1010 |         weight = torch.as_tensor(channel_weight, device=loss.device, dtype=loss.dtype).view(1, -1, 1)
 1011 |         return self._masked_mean((loss * weight).sum(dim=1), valid_mask)
 1012 | 
 1013 |     def _initial_inference_residual(self, condition, start_noise=None):
 1014 |         residual = condition.new_zeros((condition.shape[0], 2, condition.shape[-1]))
 1015 |         if self.cfm_inference_start_noise_scale > 0:
 1016 |             if start_noise is None:
 1017 |                 start_noise = torch.randn_like(residual)
 1018 |             residual = residual + self.cfm_inference_start_noise_scale * start_noise
 1019 |         return residual
 1020 | 
 1021 |     def _integrate_residual_flow(self, condition, steps=None, start_noise=None):
 1022 |         steps = int(steps or self.cfm_inference_steps)
 1023 |         steps = max(steps, 1)
 1024 |         residual = self._initial_inference_residual(condition, start_noise=start_noise)
 1025 |         dt = 1.0 / steps
 1026 |         for step in range(steps):
 1027 |             t_value = (step + 0.5) / steps
 1028 |             t = torch.full((condition.shape[0],), t_value, device=condition.device, dtype=condition.dtype)
 1029 |             residual = residual + dt * self.residual_flow(residual, condition, t)
 1030 |         return residual
 1031 | 
 1032 |     def _inference_start_noises(self, condition):
 1033 |         if self.cfm_inference_start_noise_scale <= 0:
 1034 |             return [None] * self.cfm_inference_shots
 1035 |         noise_shape = (condition.shape[0], 2, condition.shape[-1])
 1036 |         if self.cfm_inference_shot_sampling == "independent":
 1037 |             return [torch.randn(noise_shape, device=condition.device, dtype=condition.dtype) for _ in range(self.cfm_inference_shots)]
 1038 |         half_shots = self.cfm_inference_shots // 2
 1039 |         noises = [torch.randn(noise_shape, device=condition.device, dtype=condition.dtype) for _ in range(half_shots)]
 1040 |         paired_noises = []
 1041 |         for noise in noises:
 1042 |             paired_noises.extend([noise, -noise])
 1043 |         return paired_noises
 1044 | 
 1045 |     def _aggregate_inference_shots(self, shots):
 1046 |         if self.cfm_inference_shot_aggregation == "mean":
 1047 |             return shots.mean(dim=0)
 1048 |         sorted_shots = shots.sort(dim=0).values
 1049 |         shot_count = sorted_shots.shape[0]
 1050 |         mid = shot_count // 2
 1051 |         if shot_count % 2 == 1:
 1052 |             return sorted_shots[mid]
 1053 |         return 0.5 * (sorted_shots[mid - 1] + sorted_shots[mid])
 1054 | 
 1055 |     def _refine_from_base(self, noisy_audio, base_restored, baseline_hat=None):
 1056 |         condition = self._flow_condition(noisy_audio, base_restored, baseline_hat=baseline_hat)
 1057 |         if self.cfm_inference_shots > 1 and not self.training:
 1058 |             start_noises = self._inference_start_noises(condition)
 1059 |             flow_hat = torch.stack(
 1060 |                 [self._integrate_residual_flow(condition, start_noise=start_noise) for start_noise in start_noises],
 1061 |                 dim=0,
 1062 |             )
 1063 |             flow_hat = self._aggregate_inference_shots(flow_hat)
 1064 |         else:
 1065 |             flow_hat = self._integrate_residual_flow(condition)
 1066 |         clean_delta_hat = flow_hat[:, 0:1]
 1067 |         baseline_delta_hat = flow_hat[:, 1:2]
 1068 |         estimated_baseline = noisy_audio - base_restored
 1069 |         clean_low = self._baseline_projection(clean_delta_hat)
 1070 |         clean_delta_hat = clean_delta_hat - (1.0 - self.cfm_clean_lowpass_keep) * clean_low
 1071 |         if self.cfm_project_baseline_delta:
 1072 |             baseline_delta_hat = self._baseline_projection(baseline_delta_hat)
 1073 |         clean_delta_hat = self._limit_delta(clean_delta_hat, estimated_baseline, self.cfm_clean_delta_budget)
 1074 |         baseline_delta_hat = self._limit_delta(baseline_delta_hat, estimated_baseline, self.cfm_baseline_delta_budget)
 1075 |         clean_gate, baseline_gate, consistency_blend = self._cfm_gates(condition)
 1076 |         clean_path = base_restored + clean_gate * clean_delta_hat
 1077 |         baseline_path = noisy_audio - (estimated_baseline + baseline_gate * baseline_delta_hat)
 1078 |         refined = clean_path + consistency_blend * (baseline_path - clean_path)
 1079 |         return refined, flow_hat, condition, clean_path, baseline_path
 1080 | 
 1081 |     def restore_one_shot(self, noisy_audio, return_com=False):
 1082 |         restored, com_g, baseline_hat, _, _ = self._restore_components(noisy_audio)
 1083 |         refined, _, _, _, _ = self._refine_from_base(noisy_audio, restored, baseline_hat=baseline_hat)
 1084 |         if return_com:
 1085 |             return refined, com_g
 1086 |         return refined
 1087 | 
 1088 |     def restore_with_metadata(self, noisy_audio, valid_mask=None):
 1089 |         if noisy_audio.ndim == 2:
 1090 |             noisy_audio = noisy_audio.unsqueeze(1)
 1091 |         if noisy_audio.shape[1] != 1:
 1092 |             raise ValueError(
 1093 |                 f"MambAttention-STFrFT dual-path DAPP CFM expects single-lead input shaped [B, 1, T], got {noisy_audio.shape}."
 1094 |             )
 1095 |         norm_factor = self._norm_factor(noisy_audio)
 1096 |         noisy_audio_norm = noisy_audio * norm_factor
 1097 |         base_restored, _, baseline_hat, residual_delta_hat, _ = self._restore_components(noisy_audio_norm)
 1098 |         refined, flow_hat, _, _, _ = self._refine_from_base(noisy_audio_norm, base_restored, baseline_hat=baseline_hat)
 1099 |         metadata = {
 1100 |             "baseline_hat_abs_mean": baseline_hat.detach().abs().mean(dim=-1),
 1101 |             "residual_delta_abs_mean": residual_delta_hat.detach().abs().mean(dim=-1),
 1102 |             "cfm_clean_delta_abs_mean": flow_hat[:, 0:1].detach().abs().mean(dim=-1),
 1103 |             "cfm_baseline_delta_abs_mean": flow_hat[:, 1:2].detach().abs().mean(dim=-1),
 1104 |         }
 1105 |         clean_gate, baseline_gate, consistency_blend = self._cfm_gates(
 1106 |             self._flow_condition(noisy_audio_norm, base_restored, baseline_hat=baseline_hat)
 1107 |         )
 1108 |         metadata["cfm_refine_gate"] = clean_gate.detach().mean(dim=0).view(1)
 1109 |         metadata["cfm_baseline_gate"] = baseline_gate.detach().mean(dim=0).view(1)
 1110 |         metadata["cfm_consistency_blend"] = consistency_blend.detach().mean(dim=0).view(1)
 1111 |         self.last_metadata = metadata
 1112 |         return refined / norm_factor, metadata
 1113 | 
 1114 |     def _multi_resolution_stft_loss(self, clean_audio, restored_audio, valid_mask=None):
 1115 |         if self.lambda_cfm_stft <= 0:
 1116 |             return clean_audio.new_tensor(0.0)
 1117 |         loss = clean_audio.new_tensor(0.0)
 1118 |         configs = self.h.get("cfm_stft_configs", [[32, 4, 32], [64, 8, 64], [128, 16, 128]])
 1119 |         for n_fft, hop_size, win_size in configs:
 1120 |             n_fft = int(n_fft)
 1121 |             hop_size = int(hop_size)
 1122 |             win_size = int(win_size)
 1123 |             if clean_audio.shape[-1] < win_size:
 1124 |                 continue
 1125 |             clean_spec = torch.stft(
 1126 |                 clean_audio,
 1127 |                 n_fft=n_fft,
 1128 |                 hop_length=hop_size,
 1129 |                 win_length=win_size,
 1130 |                 window=torch.hann_window(win_size, device=clean_audio.device, dtype=clean_audio.dtype),
 1131 |                 center=True,
 1132 |                 pad_mode="reflect",
 1133 |                 return_complex=True,
 1134 |             )
 1135 |             restored_spec = torch.stft(
 1136 |                 restored_audio,
 1137 |                 n_fft=n_fft,
 1138 |                 hop_length=hop_size,
 1139 |                 win_length=win_size,
 1140 |                 window=torch.hann_window(win_size, device=restored_audio.device, dtype=restored_audio.dtype),
 1141 |                 center=True,
 1142 |                 pad_mode="reflect",
 1143 |                 return_complex=True,
 1144 |             )
 1145 |             loss = loss + F.smooth_l1_loss(torch.abs(restored_spec), torch.abs(clean_spec))
 1146 |         return loss / max(len(configs), 1)
 1147 | 
 1148 |     def _cfm_auxiliary_losses(self, clean_audio, noisy_audio, base_restored, refined, flow_hat, clean_path, baseline_path, valid_mask=None):
 1149 |         loss = clean_audio.new_tensor(0.0)
 1150 |         if self.lambda_cfm_recon > 0:
 1151 |             recon = F.smooth_l1_loss(refined.squeeze(1), clean_audio, reduction="none")
 1152 |             loss = loss + self.lambda_cfm_recon * self._masked_mean(recon, valid_mask)
 1153 |         if self.lambda_cfm_stft > 0:
 1154 |             loss = loss + self.lambda_cfm_stft * self._multi_resolution_stft_loss(clean_audio, refined.squeeze(1), valid_mask)
 1155 |         if self.lambda_cfm_lf > 0:
 1156 |             target_baseline = self._baseline_projection(noisy_audio - clean_audio.unsqueeze(1))
 1157 |             predicted_baseline = self._baseline_projection(noisy_audio - refined)
 1158 |             lf_loss = F.smooth_l1_loss(predicted_baseline, target_baseline, reduction="none")
 1159 |             loss = loss + self.lambda_cfm_lf * self._masked_mean(lf_loss.squeeze(1), valid_mask)
 1160 |         if self.lambda_cfm_deriv > 0:
 1161 |             clean_derivative = clean_audio[..., 1:] - clean_audio[..., :-1]
 1162 |             refined_derivative = refined.squeeze(1)[..., 1:] - refined.squeeze(1)[..., :-1]
 1163 |             deriv_loss = F.smooth_l1_loss(refined_derivative, clean_derivative, reduction="none")
 1164 |             derivative_mask = valid_mask[..., 1:] * valid_mask[..., :-1] if valid_mask is not None else None
 1165 |             loss = loss + self.lambda_cfm_deriv * self._masked_mean(deriv_loss, derivative_mask)
 1166 |         if self.lambda_cfm_residual_smooth > 0:
 1167 |             residual_curvature = flow_hat[..., 2:] - 2.0 * flow_hat[..., 1:-1] + flow_hat[..., :-2]
 1168 |             smooth_mask = valid_mask[..., 2:] * valid_mask[..., 1:-1] * valid_mask[..., :-2] if valid_mask is not None else None
 1169 |             loss = loss + self.lambda_cfm_residual_smooth * self._masked_mean(residual_curvature.abs().sum(dim=1), smooth_mask)
 1170 |         if self.lambda_cfm_clean_baseline_consistency > 0:
 1171 |             consistency = F.smooth_l1_loss(clean_path, baseline_path, reduction="none")
 1172 |             loss = loss + self.lambda_cfm_clean_baseline_consistency * self._masked_mean(consistency.squeeze(1), valid_mask)
 1173 |         return loss
 1174 | 
 1175 |     def forward(self, clean_audio, noisy_audio, valid_mask=None):
 1176 |         norm_factor = self._norm_factor(noisy_audio)
 1177 |         clean_audio = (clean_audio * norm_factor).squeeze(1)
 1178 |         noisy_audio = noisy_audio * norm_factor
 1179 |         if valid_mask is not None:
 1180 |             valid_mask = valid_mask.to(noisy_audio.device)
 1181 | 
 1182 |         base_restored, com_g, baseline_hat, residual_delta_hat, direct_restored = self._restore_components(noisy_audio)
 1183 |         base_restored = torch.nan_to_num(base_restored).clamp(-1.0e4, 1.0e4)
 1184 |         com_g = torch.nan_to_num(com_g).clamp(-1.0e4, 1.0e4)
 1185 |         baseline_hat = torch.nan_to_num(baseline_hat).clamp(-1.0e4, 1.0e4)
 1186 |         residual_delta_hat = torch.nan_to_num(residual_delta_hat).clamp(-1.0e4, 1.0e4)
 1187 |         direct_restored = torch.nan_to_num(direct_restored).clamp(-1.0e4, 1.0e4)
 1188 |         refined, flow_hat, condition, clean_path, baseline_path = self._refine_from_base(
 1189 |             noisy_audio,
 1190 |             base_restored,
 1191 |             baseline_hat=baseline_hat,
 1192 |         )
 1193 |         refined_audio = refined.squeeze(1)
 1194 |         direct_restored = direct_restored.squeeze(1)
 1195 | 
 1196 |         loss = self._ecg_loss(
 1197 |             clean_audio,
 1198 |             refined_audio,
 1199 |             norm_factor,
 1200 |             predicted_com=com_g,
 1201 |             valid_mask=valid_mask,
 1202 |         )
 1203 |         if "dual" in self.loss_fn or "dual_noise" in self.loss_fn:
 1204 |             baseline_target = noisy_audio - clean_audio.unsqueeze(1)
 1205 |             residual_target = clean_audio.unsqueeze(1) - direct_restored.unsqueeze(1)
 1206 |             baseline_loss = F.mse_loss(baseline_hat, baseline_target, reduction="none")
 1207 |             residual_loss = F.mse_loss(residual_delta_hat, residual_target, reduction="none")
 1208 |             loss = loss + self.lambda_dual_baseline * self._masked_mean(baseline_loss.squeeze(1), valid_mask)
 1209 |             loss = loss + self.lambda_dual_residual * self._masked_mean(residual_loss.squeeze(1), valid_mask)
 1210 |         if "cfm" in self.loss_fn or "flow_matching" in self.loss_fn:
 1211 |             estimated_baseline = noisy_audio - base_restored.detach()
 1212 |             true_baseline = noisy_audio - clean_audio.unsqueeze(1)
 1213 |             target_clean_residual = clean_audio.unsqueeze(1) - base_restored.detach()
 1214 |             target_baseline_residual = true_baseline - estimated_baseline
 1215 |             target_residual = torch.cat([target_clean_residual, target_baseline_residual], dim=1)
 1216 |             channel_weight = [self.lambda_cfm, self.lambda_cfm_baseline]
 1217 |             loss = loss + self._flow_matching_loss(
 1218 |                 condition.detach(),
 1219 |                 target_residual.detach(),
 1220 |                 channel_weight=channel_weight,
 1221 |                 valid_mask=valid_mask,
 1222 |             )
 1223 |             loss = loss + self._cfm_auxiliary_losses(
 1224 |                 clean_audio,

 1260 |     def _split_unet_output(self, output):
 1261 |         velocity = output[:, :2]
 1262 |         auxiliary = output[:, 2:] if output.shape[1] > 2 else None
 1263 |         return velocity, auxiliary
 1264 | 
 1265 |     def _flow_matching_loss(self, condition, target_residual, channel_weight=1.0, valid_mask=None):
 1266 |         start = self._sample_flow_start(target_residual)
 1267 |         t = torch.rand((target_residual.shape[0],), device=target_residual.device)
 1268 |         t_view = t.view(-1, 1, 1)
 1269 |         bridge_noise = self.cfm_bridge_noise_scale * torch.sin(torch.pi * t_view) * torch.randn_like(target_residual)
 1270 |         residual_state = (1.0 - t_view) * start + t_view * target_residual + bridge_noise
 1271 |         target_velocity = target_residual - start
 1272 |         predicted_velocity, _ = self._split_unet_output(self.residual_flow(residual_state, condition, t))
 1273 |         loss = F.mse_loss(predicted_velocity, target_velocity, reduction="none")
 1274 |         weight = torch.as_tensor(channel_weight, device=loss.device, dtype=loss.dtype).view(1, -1, 1)
 1275 |         return self._masked_mean((loss * weight).sum(dim=1), valid_mask)
 1276 | 
 1277 |     def _integrate_residual_flow(self, condition, steps=None, start_noise=None):
 1278 |         steps = int(steps or self.cfm_inference_steps)
 1279 |         steps = max(steps, 1)
 1280 |         residual = self._initial_inference_residual(condition, start_noise=start_noise)
 1281 |         dt = 1.0 / steps
 1282 |         for step in range(steps):
 1283 |             t_value = (step + 0.5) / steps
 1284 |             t = torch.full((condition.shape[0],), t_value, device=condition.device, dtype=condition.dtype)
 1285 |             velocity, _ = self._split_unet_output(self.residual_flow(residual, condition, t))
 1286 |             residual = residual + dt * velocity
 1287 |         return residual
 1288 | 
 1289 |     def _noise_auxiliary_loss(self, condition, clean_audio, noisy_audio, base_restored, valid_mask=None):
 1290 |         if self.cfm_unet_aux_channels < 2 or self.lambda_ecg_noise_aux + self.lambda_gaussian_noise_aux <= 0:
 1291 |             return clean_audio.new_tensor(0.0)
 1292 |         true_baseline = noisy_audio - clean_audio.unsqueeze(1)
 1293 |         gaussian_noise = torch.randn_like(true_baseline)
 1294 |         t = torch.rand((true_baseline.shape[0],), device=true_baseline.device)
 1295 |         t_view = t.view(-1, 1, 1)
 1296 |         sigma = self.gaussian_aux_scale * torch.sqrt((t_view * (1.0 - t_view)).clamp_min(1.0e-8))
 1297 |         xt = clean_audio.unsqueeze(1) + t_view * true_baseline + sigma * gaussian_noise
 1298 |         aux_state = torch.cat([xt - base_restored.detach(), noisy_audio - xt], dim=1)
 1299 |         _, auxiliary = self._split_unet_output(self.residual_flow(aux_state, condition.detach(), t))
 1300 |         if auxiliary is None or auxiliary.shape[1] < 2:
 1301 |             return clean_audio.new_tensor(0.0)
 1302 |         baseline_loss = F.mse_loss(auxiliary[:, 0:1], true_baseline.detach(), reduction="none")
 1303 |         gaussian_loss = F.mse_loss(auxiliary[:, 1:2], gaussian_noise.detach(), reduction="none")
 1304 |         loss = clean_audio.new_tensor(0.0)
 1305 |         if self.lambda_ecg_noise_aux > 0:
 1306 |             loss = loss + self.lambda_ecg_noise_aux * self._masked_mean(baseline_loss.squeeze(1), valid_mask)
 1307 |         if self.lambda_gaussian_noise_aux > 0:
 1308 |             loss = loss + self.lambda_gaussian_noise_aux * self._masked_mean(gaussian_loss.squeeze(1), valid_mask)
 1309 |         return loss

 1312 |         return (
 1313 |             torch.nan_to_num(base_restored).clamp(-1.0e4, 1.0e4),

 1317 |             torch.nan_to_num(direct_restored).clamp(-1.0e4, 1.0e4),
 1318 |         )

SOURCE src/models/lstm_v12/__init__.py:21 — BoundedTemporalGateOffsets
   21 | class BoundedTemporalGateOffsets(nn.Module):
   22 |     """Three local logit offsets, bounded before optional moving averaging."""
   23 | 
   24 |     def __init__(self, channels=6, hidden=16, kernel_size=7, dilations=(1, 4),
   25 |                  bound=0.5, smooth_kernel=33, smoothing=True):
   26 |         super().__init__()
   27 |         if hidden < 1 or kernel_size < 1 or kernel_size % 2 != 1:
   28 |             raise ValueError("Temporal gate hidden width must be positive; kernel must be positive and odd.")
   29 |         if smooth_kernel < 1 or smooth_kernel % 2 != 1:
   30 |             raise ValueError("v12_gate_smooth_kernel must be positive and odd.")
   31 |         if not math.isfinite(bound) or bound <= 0:
   32 |             raise ValueError("v12_gate_offset_bound must be finite and positive.")
   33 |         if not dilations or any(int(d) != d or d < 1 for d in dilations):
   34 |             raise ValueError("v12_gate_dilations must contain positive integers.")
   35 |         self.bound = float(bound)
   36 |         self.smooth_kernel = int(smooth_kernel)
   37 |         self.smoothing = bool(smoothing)
   38 |         layers = []
   39 |         for dilation in dilations:
   40 |             layers.extend([
   41 |                 nn.Conv1d(channels, hidden, kernel_size,
   42 |                           padding=int(dilation) * (kernel_size // 2), dilation=int(dilation)),
   43 |                 nn.SiLU(),
   44 |             ])
   45 |             channels = hidden
   46 |         layers.append(nn.Conv1d(hidden, 3, 1))
   47 |         self.net = nn.Sequential(*layers)
   48 |         nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].weight)
   49 |         nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].bias)
   50 | 
   51 |     def smooth(self, values):
   52 |         if not self.smoothing or self.smooth_kernel == 1:
   53 |             return values
   54 |         radius = self.smooth_kernel // 2
   55 |         return F.avg_pool1d(F.pad(values, (radius, radius), mode="replicate"),
   56 |                             self.smooth_kernel, stride=1)
   57 | 
   58 |     def forward(self, condition):
   59 |         return self.bound * self.smooth(torch.tanh(self.net(condition)))

   62 | class TemporalGateV12Core(UNetResidualFlowDualPathDAPPMambAttentionCore):
   63 |     def __init__(self, config):
   64 |         # All original modules are initialized in their original order. Forking
   65 |         # only the new branch also preserves the subsequent training RNG stream.
   66 |         super().__init__(config, block_cls=LightweightLSTMContextBlock)
   67 |         h = self.h
   68 |         self.use_local_gate = bool(h.get("v12_local_gate", True))
   69 |         self.use_dual_head = bool(h.get("v12_dual_head", True))
   70 |         self.tail_final_weight = float(h.get("v12_tail_weight", 0.0))
   71 |         self.tail_ramp_epochs = int(h.get("v12_tail_ramp_epochs", 10))
   72 |         self.tail_topk = int(h.get("mad_topk", 8))
   73 |         if not math.isfinite(self.tail_final_weight) or self.tail_final_weight < 0:
   74 |             raise ValueError("v12_tail_weight must be finite and nonnegative.")
   75 |         if self.tail_ramp_epochs < 1 or self.tail_topk < 1:
   76 |             raise ValueError("v12_tail_ramp_epochs and mad_topk must be positive.")
   77 |         if self.tail_final_weight > 0 and "max" in self.loss_fn:
   78 |             raise ValueError("Delayed top-k loss cannot be combined with the legacy 'max' loss token.")
   79 |         self.current_epoch = 0
   80 |         self.total_epochs = None
   81 |         with torch.random.fork_rng(devices=[]):
   82 |             self.temporal_gate = BoundedTemporalGateOffsets(
   83 |                 channels=self.cfm_condition_channels,
   84 |                 hidden=int(h.get("v12_gate_hidden", 16)),
   85 |                 kernel_size=int(h.get("v12_gate_kernel_size", 7)),
   86 |                 dilations=tuple(h.get("v12_gate_dilations", [1, 4])),
   87 |                 bound=float(h.get("v12_gate_offset_bound", 0.5)),
   88 |                 smooth_kernel=int(h.get("v12_gate_smooth_kernel", 33)),
   89 |                 smoothing=bool(h.get("v12_gate_smoothing", True)),
   90 |             )
   91 | 
   92 |         # Ablation removal follows construction, so surviving parameters match.
   93 |         if not self.use_local_gate:
   94 |             self.temporal_gate = None
   95 |         if not bool(h.get("v12_feature_dapp", True)):
   96 |             self.feature_dapp = nn.Identity()
   97 |         if not bool(h.get("v12_unet_dapp", True)):
   98 |             self.residual_flow.first_skip_dapp = nn.Identity()
   99 |         if not bool(h.get("v12_resconv", True)):
  100 |             for block in self.tsc_blocks:
  101 |                 block.context = nn.Identity()
  102 |         if not self.use_dual_head:
  103 |             self.dual_noise_head = None
  104 |             # The head has two output channels (baseline and clean residual).
  105 |             # Removing both also removes both associated supervision terms.
  106 |             self.loss_fn = [token for token in self.loss_fn if token not in {"dual", "dual_noise"}]
  107 | 
  108 |     def set_epoch(self, epoch, total_epochs):
  109 |         """Set a one-based training epoch; ramp over the final configured epochs."""
  110 |         if int(epoch) != epoch or int(total_epochs) != total_epochs or not 1 <= epoch <= total_epochs:
  111 |             raise ValueError("Require integer epochs with 1 <= epoch <= total_epochs.")
  112 |         self.current_epoch, self.total_epochs = int(epoch), int(total_epochs)
  113 | 
  114 |     def tail_weight(self):
  115 |         # Validation always uses the terminal objective, independent of the
  116 |         # epoch, for checkpoint and ReduceLROnPlateau comparisons.
  117 |         if not self.training:
  118 |             return self.tail_final_weight
  119 |         if self.total_epochs is None:
  120 |             return 0.0
  121 |         ramp = min(self.tail_ramp_epochs, self.total_epochs)
  122 |         progress = (self.current_epoch - (self.total_epochs - ramp)) / ramp
  123 |         return self.tail_final_weight * max(0.0, min(1.0, progress))
  124 | 
  125 |     def _cfm_gates(self, condition):
  126 |         if self.temporal_gate is None:
  127 |             return super()._cfm_gates(condition)
  128 |         if self.cfm_adaptive_gate is not None:
  129 |             stats = torch.cat([condition.abs().mean(-1), condition.std(-1)], dim=-1)
  130 |             logits = self.cfm_adaptive_gate.net(stats).unsqueeze(-1)
  131 |         else:
  132 |             logits = torch.stack([self.cfm_refine_gate_raw, self.cfm_baseline_gate_raw,
  133 |                                   self.cfm_consistency_blend_raw]).view(1, 3, 1)
  134 |         gates = torch.sigmoid(logits + self.temporal_gate(condition))
  135 |         return (self.cfm_refine_gate_max * gates[:, 0:1],
  136 |                 self.cfm_baseline_gate_max * gates[:, 1:2],
  137 |                 self.cfm_consistency_blend_max * gates[:, 2:3])
  138 | 
  139 |     def _predict_dual_noise(self, encoded, length):
  140 |         if self.use_dual_head:
  141 |             return super()._predict_dual_noise(encoded, length)
  142 |         zeros = encoded.new_zeros((encoded.shape[0], 1, length))
  143 |         return zeros, zeros
  144 | 
  145 |     def _restore_components(self, noisy_audio):
  146 |         restored, spectrum, baseline, delta, direct = super()._restore_components(noisy_audio)
  147 |         if not self.use_dual_head:
  148 |             noisy = noisy_audio.unsqueeze(1) if noisy_audio.ndim == 2 else noisy_audio
  149 |             baseline = noisy - restored
  150 |         return restored, spectrum, baseline, delta, direct
  151 | 
  152 |     def _tail_loss(self, clean_audio, restored_audio, valid_mask=None):
  153 |         # Match the existing v7_max normalized-domain absolute-error definition.
  154 |         error = (restored_audio - clean_audio).abs()
  155 |         count = min(self.tail_topk, error.shape[-1])
  156 |         if valid_mask is None:
  157 |             return error.topk(count, dim=-1).values.mean()
  158 |         mask = valid_mask.to(device=error.device, dtype=torch.bool)
  159 |         if mask.ndim == 3:
  160 |             mask = mask.squeeze(1)
  161 |         values = error.masked_fill(~mask, 0.0).topk(count, dim=-1).values
  162 |         valid_count = mask.sum(dim=-1).clamp(max=count)
  163 |         per_window = values.sum(dim=-1) / valid_count.clamp_min(1)
  164 |         # Completely padded windows contribute neither error nor sample count.
  165 |         return per_window.sum() / (valid_count > 0).sum().clamp_min(1)
  166 | 
  167 |     def _ecg_loss(self, clean_audio, restored_audio, norm_factor,
  168 |                   predicted_com=None, aux_history=None, valid_mask=None):
  169 |         loss = super()._ecg_loss(clean_audio, restored_audio, norm_factor,
  170 |                                  predicted_com=predicted_com, aux_history=aux_history,
  171 |                                  valid_mask=valid_mask)
  172 |         weight = self.tail_weight()
  173 |         if weight > 0:
  174 |             loss = loss + weight * self._tail_loss(clean_audio, restored_audio, valid_mask)
  175 |         return loss
  176 | 
  177 |     def forward(self, clean_audio, noisy_audio, valid_mask=None):
  178 |         if noisy_audio.ndim == 2:
  179 |             noisy_audio = noisy_audio.unsqueeze(1)
  180 |         if clean_audio.ndim == 2:
  181 |             clean_audio = clean_audio.unsqueeze(1)
  182 |         if noisy_audio.ndim != 3 or noisy_audio.shape[1] != 1 or clean_audio.shape != noisy_audio.shape:
  183 |             raise ValueError("v12 expects matching single-lead inputs [B, 1, T] or [B, T].")
  184 |         if valid_mask is not None:
  185 |             if valid_mask.ndim == 3 and valid_mask.shape[1] == 1:
  186 |                 valid_mask = valid_mask.squeeze(1)
  187 |             if valid_mask.shape != noisy_audio[:, 0].shape:
  188 |                 raise ValueError("valid_mask must have shape [B, T] or [B, 1, T].")
  189 |         return super().forward(clean_audio, noisy_audio, valid_mask=valid_mask)
  190 | 
  191 |     def restore_with_metadata(self, noisy_audio, valid_mask=None):
  192 |         if noisy_audio.ndim == 2:
  193 |             noisy_audio = noisy_audio.unsqueeze(1)
  194 |         if noisy_audio.ndim != 3 or noisy_audio.shape[1] != 1:
  195 |             raise ValueError("v12 expects single-lead input [B, 1, T] or [B, T].")
  196 |         factor = self._norm_factor(noisy_audio)
  197 |         noisy = noisy_audio * factor
  198 |         coarse, _, baseline, delta, _ = self._restore_components(noisy)
  199 |         restored, flow, condition, _, _ = self._refine_from_base(noisy, coarse, baseline_hat=baseline)
  200 |         metadata = {
  201 |             "baseline_hat_abs_mean": baseline.detach().abs().mean(-1),
  202 |             "residual_delta_abs_mean": delta.detach().abs().mean(-1),
  203 |             "cfm_clean_delta_abs_mean": flow[:, :1].detach().abs().mean(-1),
  204 |             "cfm_baseline_delta_abs_mean": flow[:, 1:2].detach().abs().mean(-1),
  205 |         }
  206 |         gates = self._cfm_gates(condition)
  207 |         for name, values in zip(("cfm_refine_gate", "cfm_baseline_gate", "cfm_consistency_blend"), gates):
  208 |             values = values.detach()
  209 |             metadata[name] = values.mean().view(1)
  210 |             metadata[name + "_min"] = values.amin().view(1)
  211 |             metadata[name + "_max"] = values.amax().view(1)
  212 |             metadata[name + "_temporal_std"] = values.std(dim=-1, unbiased=False).mean().view(1)
  213 |         self.last_metadata = metadata
  214 |         return restored / factor, metadata

   18 |     """Preserve v12 parameters/inference; reinforce amplitude and raw direction."""

   20 |     def __init__(self, config):
   21 |         settings = dict(config["model"])
   22 |         settings.setdefault("v12_local_gate", True)
   23 |         settings.setdefault("v12_tail_weight", 0.01)
   24 |         extra_mse = float(settings.get("v13_extra_mse_weight", 0.25))
   25 |         raw_cos = float(settings.get("v13_raw_cos_weight", 0.01))
   26 |         ramp = settings.get("v13_ramp_epochs", 10)
   27 |         for name, weight in (("v13_extra_mse_weight", extra_mse),
   28 |                              ("v13_raw_cos_weight", raw_cos)):
   29 |             if not math.isfinite(weight) or weight < 0:
   30 |                 raise ValueError(f"{name} must be finite and nonnegative.")
   31 |         if (isinstance(ramp, bool) or not isinstance(ramp, (int, float))
   32 |                 or not math.isfinite(ramp) or int(ramp) != ramp or ramp < 1):
   33 |             raise ValueError("v13_ramp_epochs must be a positive integer.")
   34 |         if raw_cos > 0 and "cos" in settings.get("loss_fn", "").split("+"):
   35 |             raise ValueError("Do not combine v13 raw cosine with the legacy centered 'cos' loss.")
   36 |         super().__init__({"model": settings})
   37 |         self.extra_mse_final_weight = extra_mse
   38 |         self.raw_cos_final_weight = raw_cos
   39 |         self.metric_ramp_epochs = int(ramp)
   40 | 
   41 |     def metric_weights(self):
   42 |         """Validation uses terminal weights, independent of the training epoch."""
   43 |         if not self.training:
   44 |             scale = 1.0
   45 |         elif self.total_epochs is None:
   46 |             scale = 0.0
   47 |         else:
   48 |             ramp = min(self.metric_ramp_epochs, self.total_epochs)
   49 |             progress = (self.current_epoch - (self.total_epochs - ramp)) / ramp
   50 |             scale = max(0.0, min(1.0, progress))
   51 |         return {"extra_mse": scale * self.extra_mse_final_weight,
   52 |                 "raw_cos": scale * self.raw_cos_final_weight}
   53 | 
   54 |     @staticmethod
   55 |     def _metric_inputs(clean_audio, restored_audio, norm_factor, valid_mask=None):
   56 |         # The official scores are computed in original amplitude, even if the
   57 |         # network was configured to normalize its input. Promote half precision
   58 |         # so the official cosine epsilon does not underflow to zero.
   59 |         dtype = torch.float32 if restored_audio.dtype in (torch.float16, torch.bfloat16) else restored_audio.dtype
   60 |         factor = norm_factor.squeeze(-1).to(device=restored_audio.device, dtype=dtype)
   61 |         clean = clean_audio.to(dtype=dtype) / factor
   62 |         restored = restored_audio.to(dtype=dtype) / factor
   63 |         mask = None
   64 |         if valid_mask is not None:
   65 |             mask = valid_mask.to(device=restored.device, dtype=torch.bool)
   66 |             if mask.ndim == 3 and mask.shape[1] == 1:
   67 |                 mask = mask.squeeze(1)
   68 |             if mask.shape != restored.shape:
   69 |                 raise ValueError("valid_mask must match the metric inputs [B, T] or [B, 1, T].")
   70 |             clean = clean.masked_fill(~mask, 0.0)
   71 |             restored = restored.masked_fill(~mask, 0.0)
   72 |         return clean, restored, mask
   73 | 
   74 |     def _extra_mse_loss(self, clean_audio, restored_audio, norm_factor, valid_mask=None):
   75 |         clean, restored, mask = self._metric_inputs(clean_audio, restored_audio, norm_factor, valid_mask)
   76 |         return self._masked_mean((restored - clean).square(), mask)
   77 | 
   78 |     def _raw_cosine_loss(self, clean_audio, restored_audio, norm_factor, valid_mask=None):
   79 |         """Uncentered per-window cosine, with the official additive epsilon.
   80 | 
   81 |         All-masked and zero-clean-energy windows have no reference direction and
   82 |         are excluded from this training term; MSE still supervises zero-clean
   83 |         windows. Official evaluation is unchanged. vector_norm has a finite
   84 |         backward at zero, unlike a naive sqrt(sum(square)) implementation.
   85 |         """
   86 |         clean, restored, _ = self._metric_inputs(clean_audio, restored_audio, norm_factor, valid_mask)
   87 |         clean_norm = torch.linalg.vector_norm(clean, dim=-1)
   88 |         restored_norm = torch.linalg.vector_norm(restored, dim=-1)
   89 |         cosine = (clean * restored).sum(dim=-1) / (clean_norm * restored_norm + 1.0e-10)
   90 |         eligible = clean_norm > 0
   91 |         error = torch.where(eligible, 1.0 - cosine, torch.zeros_like(cosine))
   92 |         return error.sum() / eligible.sum().clamp_min(1)
   93 | 
   94 |     def _ecg_loss(self, clean_audio, restored_audio, norm_factor,
   95 |                   predicted_com=None, aux_history=None, valid_mask=None):
   96 |         loss = super()._ecg_loss(clean_audio, restored_audio, norm_factor,
   97 |                                  predicted_com=predicted_com, aux_history=aux_history,
   98 |                                  valid_mask=valid_mask)
   99 |         weights = self.metric_weights()
  100 |         # Avoid even zero-weight additions: the neither ablation preserves the
  101 |         # exact v12 delayed-top-k loss and RNG trajectory, not just its formula.
  102 |         if weights["extra_mse"] > 0:
  103 |             loss = loss + weights["extra_mse"] * self._extra_mse_loss(
  104 |                 clean_audio, restored_audio, norm_factor, valid_mask)
  105 |         if weights["raw_cos"] > 0:
  106 |             loss = loss + weights["raw_cos"] * self._raw_cosine_loss(
  107 |                 clean_audio, restored_audio, norm_factor, valid_mask)
  108 |         return loss

SOURCE src/models/lstm_v16/__init__.py:32 — GateAdapter
   32 | class GateAdapter(nn.Module):
   33 |     def __init__(self, mode="correction", hidden=24, bound=0.5):
   34 |         super().__init__()
   35 |         if mode not in {"constant", "condition", "correction"}:
   36 |             raise ValueError("v16_gate_mode must be constant, condition, or correction.")
   37 |         if type(hidden) is not int or hidden < 1:
   38 |             raise ValueError("v16_gate_hidden must be a positive integer.")
   39 |         if isinstance(bound, bool) or not isinstance(bound, (int, float)) or not math.isfinite(bound) or bound <= 0:
   40 |             raise ValueError("v16_gate_logit_bound must be finite and positive.")
   41 |         self.mode, self.bound = mode, float(bound)
   42 |         if mode == "constant":
   43 |             self.offset = nn.Parameter(torch.zeros(3))
   44 |         else:
   45 |             # B and C have identical parameter shapes and initialization. Do not
   46 |             # normalize across feature slots: zeros in B are an intentional mask.
   47 |             self.net = nn.Sequential(nn.Linear(len(FEATURE_NAMES), hidden), nn.SiLU(), nn.Linear(hidden, 3))
   48 |             nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].weight)
   49 |             nn.init.zeros_(self.net[-1].bias)
   50 | 
   51 |     def forward(self, features):
   52 |         features = features.detach()
   53 |         if self.mode == "constant":
   54 |             raw = self.offset.unsqueeze(0).expand(features.shape[0], -1)
   55 |         else:
   56 |             if self.mode == "condition":
   57 |                 features = torch.cat((features[:, :CONDITION_FEATURE_COUNT],
   58 |                                       torch.zeros_like(features[:, CONDITION_FEATURE_COUNT:])), dim=1)
   59 |             raw = self.net(features)
   60 |         return self.bound * torch.tanh(raw)
   61 | 
   62 |     def is_zero_output(self):
   63 |         tensors = (self.offset,) if self.mode == "constant" else (self.net[-1].weight, self.net[-1].bias)
   64 |         return all(torch.count_nonzero(value).item() == 0 for value in tensors)

  102 |     def _restore_components(self, noisy_audio):
  103 |         with torch.no_grad():
  104 |             return tuple(value.detach() for value in super()._restore_components(noisy_audio))

  106 |     def _base_gate_logits(self, condition):
  107 |         with torch.no_grad():
  108 |             if self.cfm_adaptive_gate is not None:
  109 |                 stats = torch.cat((condition.abs().mean(-1), condition.std(-1)), dim=-1)
  110 |                 return self.cfm_adaptive_gate.net(stats).detach()
  111 |             return torch.stack((self.cfm_refine_gate_raw, self.cfm_baseline_gate_raw,
  112 |                                 self.cfm_consistency_blend_raw)).view(1, 3).expand(condition.shape[0], -1).detach()
  113 | 
  114 |     def _project_and_limit(self, raw, reference):
  115 |         clean = raw[:, :1]
  116 |         clean = clean - (1.0 - self.cfm_clean_lowpass_keep) * self._baseline_projection(clean)
  117 |         baseline = self._baseline_projection(raw[:, 1:2]) if self.cfm_project_baseline_delta else raw[:, 1:2]
  118 |         projected = torch.cat((clean, baseline), dim=1)
  119 |         reference_level = (reference.abs().mean(-1, keepdim=True)
  120 |                            + 0.25 * reference.std(-1, keepdim=True)).clamp_min(1e-6)
  121 |         scales, limited = [], []
  122 |         for delta, budget in ((clean, self.cfm_clean_delta_budget), (baseline, self.cfm_baseline_delta_budget)):
  123 |             scale = ((float(budget) * reference_level / delta.abs().mean(-1, keepdim=True).clamp_min(1e-6)).clamp(max=1.0)
  124 |                      if budget > 0 else torch.ones_like(reference_level))
  125 |             scales.append(scale)
  126 |             limited.append(self._limit_delta(delta, reference, budget))
  127 |         return projected, torch.cat(limited, dim=1), torch.cat(scales, dim=1)
  128 | 
  129 |     def _adapter_features(self, condition, raw, projected, limited, scales):
  130 |         values = [condition.abs().mean(-1), condition.std(-1)]
  131 |         for stage in (raw, projected, limited):
  132 |             values.extend((stage.abs().mean(-1), stage.std(-1)))
  133 |         values.extend((scales.squeeze(-1), (raw[:, :1] + raw[:, 1:2]).abs().mean(-1),
  134 |                        (condition[:, 3:4] - condition[:, 2:3]).abs().mean(-1)))
  135 |         return torch.cat(values, dim=1).detach()
  136 | 
  137 |     def refinement_parts(self, noisy, base, baseline_hat=None):
  138 |         if noisy.ndim == 2:
  139 |             noisy = noisy.unsqueeze(1)
  140 |         if base.ndim == 2:
  141 |             base = base.unsqueeze(1)
  142 |         condition = self._flow_condition(noisy, base, baseline_hat=baseline_hat)
  143 |         raw = self._integrate_residual_flow(condition)
  144 |         projected, limited, scales = self._project_and_limit(raw, noisy - base)
  145 |         features = self._adapter_features(condition, raw, projected, limited, scales)
  146 |         logits = self._base_gate_logits(condition)
  147 |         offsets = self.gate_adapter(features)
  148 |         maxima = logits.new_tensor((self.cfm_refine_gate_max, self.cfm_baseline_gate_max,
  149 |                                     self.cfm_consistency_blend_max)).view(1, 3)
  150 |         gates = (maxima * torch.sigmoid(logits + offsets)).unsqueeze(-1)
  151 |         clean_gate, baseline_gate, blend = gates.split(1, dim=1)
  152 |         gated = torch.cat((clean_gate * limited[:, :1], baseline_gate * limited[:, 1:2]), dim=1)
  153 |         # Preserve original operation order for exact zero-offset parity.
  154 |         clean_path = base + gated[:, :1]
  155 |         baseline_path = noisy - ((noisy - base) + gated[:, 1:2])
  156 |         final = clean_path + blend * (baseline_path - clean_path)
  157 |         return {"final": final, "raw_flow": raw, "condition": condition,
  158 |                 "projected_flow": projected, "limited_flow": limited, "budget_scales": scales,
  159 |                 "gated_flow": gated,
  160 |                 "effective_flow": torch.cat(((1.0 - blend) * gated[:, :1], -blend * gated[:, 1:2]), dim=1),
  161 |                 "clean_path": clean_path, "baseline_path": baseline_path,
  162 |                 "gates": gates, "base_logits": logits, "adapter_offsets": offsets,
  163 |                 "adapter_features": features}
  164 | 
  165 |     def _refine_from_base(self, noisy_audio, base_restored, baseline_hat=None):
  166 |         parts = self.refinement_parts(noisy_audio, base_restored, baseline_hat)
  167 |         return tuple(parts[key] for key in ("final", "raw_flow", "condition", "clean_path", "baseline_path"))

  185 |     def _cfm_auxiliary_losses(self, clean_audio, noisy_audio, base_restored, refined,
  186 |                              flow_hat, clean_path, baseline_path, valid_mask=None):
  187 |         value = super()._cfm_auxiliary_losses(clean_audio, noisy_audio, base_restored, refined,
  188 |                                              flow_hat, clean_path, baseline_path, valid_mask)
  189 |         self.loss_component_tensors["cfm_auxiliary"] = value
  190 |         # These diagnostic subterms overlap the aggregate; they are floats only
  191 |         # and do not keep a duplicate autograd graph or invoke stochastic code.
  192 |         with torch.no_grad():
  193 |             terms = {}
  194 |             if self.lambda_cfm_recon > 0:
  195 |                 terms["final_reconstruction"] = self.lambda_cfm_recon * self._masked_mean(
  196 |                     F.smooth_l1_loss(refined.squeeze(1), clean_audio, reduction="none"), valid_mask)
  197 |             if self.lambda_cfm_lf > 0:
  198 |                 target = self._baseline_projection(noisy_audio - clean_audio.unsqueeze(1))
  199 |                 predicted = self._baseline_projection(noisy_audio - refined)
  200 |                 terms["low_frequency"] = self.lambda_cfm_lf * self._masked_mean(
  201 |                     F.smooth_l1_loss(predicted, target, reduction="none").squeeze(1), valid_mask)
  202 |             if self.lambda_cfm_deriv > 0:
  203 |                 derivative_mask = valid_mask[..., 1:] * valid_mask[..., :-1] if valid_mask is not None else None
  204 |                 terms["derivative"] = self.lambda_cfm_deriv * self._masked_mean(
  205 |                     F.smooth_l1_loss(refined.squeeze(1).diff(dim=-1), clean_audio.diff(dim=-1), reduction="none"),
  206 |                     derivative_mask)
  207 |             if self.lambda_cfm_residual_smooth > 0:
  208 |                 smooth_mask = (valid_mask[..., 2:] * valid_mask[..., 1:-1] * valid_mask[..., :-2]
  209 |                                if valid_mask is not None else None)
  210 |                 curvature = flow_hat[..., 2:] - 2.0 * flow_hat[..., 1:-1] + flow_hat[..., :-2]
  211 |                 terms["raw_flow_smoothness"] = self.lambda_cfm_residual_smooth * self._masked_mean(
  212 |                     curvature.abs().sum(dim=1), smooth_mask)
  213 |             if self.lambda_cfm_clean_baseline_consistency > 0:
  214 |                 terms["path_consistency"] = self.lambda_cfm_clean_baseline_consistency * self._masked_mean(
  215 |                     F.smooth_l1_loss(clean_path, baseline_path, reduction="none").squeeze(1), valid_mask)
  216 |             for name, term in terms.items():
  217 |                 self._loss_detail["detail_cfm_" + name] = float(term)
  218 |             if self.lambda_cfm_stft > 0:
  219 |                 self._loss_detail["detail_cfm_stft_remainder"] = float(value.detach() - sum(terms.values()))
  220 |         return value
  221 | 
  222 |     def forward(self, clean_audio, noisy_audio, valid_mask=None):
  223 |         # Discard the previous batch graph before computing the next batch.
  224 |         self.loss_component_tensors = {}
  225 |         self._loss_detail = {}
  226 |         if valid_mask is not None and valid_mask.ndim == 3:
  227 |             valid_mask = valid_mask.squeeze(1)
  228 |         total = super().forward(clean_audio, noisy_audio, valid_mask=valid_mask)
  229 |         # Stage one (including both heads) is always frozen. Detach the
  230 |         # subtraction diagnostic to avoid reporting cancellation roundoff as
  231 |         # a trainable dual-head gradient.
  232 |         self.loss_component_tensors["dual_noise_remainder"] = (total - sum(self.loss_component_tensors.values())).detach()
  233 |         self.loss_components = {name: float(value.detach()) for name, value in self.loss_component_tensors.items()}
  234 |         self.loss_components.update(self._loss_detail)
  235 |         self.loss_components["total_loss"] = float(total.detach())
  236 |         return total

  238 |     def restore_with_metadata(self, noisy_audio, valid_mask=None):
  239 |         if noisy_audio.ndim == 2:
  240 |             noisy_audio = noisy_audio.unsqueeze(1)
  241 |         if noisy_audio.ndim != 3 or noisy_audio.shape[1] != 1:
  242 |             raise ValueError("v16 expects single-lead [B,1,T] input.")
  243 |         factor = self._norm_factor(noisy_audio)
  244 |         noisy = noisy_audio * factor
  245 |         base, _, baseline, _, _ = self._restore_components(noisy)
  246 |         parts = self.refinement_parts(noisy, base, baseline)
  247 |         metadata = {name: parts["gates"][:, index].detach().mean().view(1)
  248 |                     for index, name in enumerate(("cfm_refine_gate", "cfm_baseline_gate", "cfm_consistency_blend"))}
  249 |         self.last_metadata = metadata
  250 |         return parts["final"] / factor, metadata

  269 |     def configure_finetuning(self, scope="adapter", lr_adapter=1e-4, lr_flow=1e-5):
  270 |         if scope not in {"adapter", "adapter_flow"}:
  271 |             raise ValueError("scope must be adapter or adapter_flow.")
  272 |         rates = {"adapter": lr_adapter, "flow": lr_flow}
  273 |         if any(isinstance(value, bool) or not isinstance(value, (int, float)) or not math.isfinite(value) or value <= 0
  274 |                for value in rates.values()):
  275 |             raise ValueError("Learning rates must be finite and positive.")
  276 |         groups = {name: [] for name in rates}
  277 |         for name, parameter in self.named_parameters():
  278 |             group = ("adapter" if name.startswith("core.gate_adapter.") else
  279 |                      "flow" if scope == "adapter_flow" and name.startswith("core.residual_flow.") else None)
  280 |             parameter.requires_grad_(group is not None)
  281 |             if group is None:
  282 |                 parameter.grad = None
  283 |             else:
  284 |                 groups[group].append(parameter)
  285 |         self.core.finetuning_scope = scope
  286 |         self.train(self.training)
  287 |         return [{"name": name, "params": parameters, "lr": float(rates[name])}
  288 |                 for name, parameters in groups.items() if parameters]
  289 | 
  290 |     def finetuning_summary(self):
  291 |         counts = {"adapter": 0, "flow": 0, "frozen": 0}
  292 |         for name, parameter in self.named_parameters():
  293 |             group = "frozen" if not parameter.requires_grad else ("adapter" if name.startswith("core.gate_adapter.") else "flow")
  294 |             counts[group] += parameter.numel()
  295 |         return {"scope": self.core.finetuning_scope, "gate_mode": self.core.gate_adapter.mode,
  296 |                 "logit_bound": self.core.gate_adapter.bound, "parameter_counts": counts,
  297 |                 "trainable_parameters": counts["adapter"] + counts["flow"], "total_parameters": sum(counts.values())}
  298 | 
  299 |     @torch.no_grad()
  300 |     def stages(self, noisy):
  301 |         """Return deterministic stages; all waveforms except condition are raw amplitude.
  302 | 
  303 |         Gates/scales have [B,C,1], features/logits [B,C], scalar diagnostics [B].
  304 |         effective_flow includes mixing and the baseline channel's minus sign.
  305 |         Counterfactual budget bypasses reuse the actual gates, without refitting.
  306 |         """
  307 |         if noisy.ndim == 2:
  308 |             noisy = noisy.unsqueeze(1)
  309 |         if noisy.ndim != 3 or noisy.shape[1] != 1 or not torch.isfinite(noisy).all():
  310 |             raise ValueError("stages requires finite single-lead [B,1,T] or [B,T] input.")
  311 |         was_training = self.training
  312 |         self.eval()
  313 |         try:
  314 |             core = self.core
  315 |             factor = core._norm_factor(noisy)
  316 |             if not torch.isfinite(factor).all() or not (factor > 0).all():
  317 |                 raise ValueError("Invalid normalization factor.")
  318 |             normalized = noisy * factor
  319 |             base, _, baseline, dual_residual, direct = core._restore_components(normalized)
  320 |             parts = core.refinement_parts(normalized, base, baseline)
  321 |             gates, scales = parts["gates"], parts["budget_scales"]
  322 |             diagnostics = {}
  323 |             for index, name in enumerate(("clean_gate", "baseline_gate", "consistency_blend")):
  324 |                 diagnostics[name] = gates[:, index, 0]
  325 |                 diagnostics[name + "_mean"] = gates[:, index, 0]
  326 |                 diagnostics[name + "_logit_offset"] = parts["adapter_offsets"][:, index]
  327 |             for index, name in enumerate(("clean", "baseline")):
  328 |                 diagnostics[name + "_budget_scale"] = scales[:, index, 0]
  329 |                 diagnostics[name + "_budget_limited"] = (scales[:, index, 0] < 1).to(base.dtype)
  330 |                 for stage in ("raw", "projected", "limited", "gated", "effective"):
  331 |                     wave = parts[stage + "_flow"][:, index:index + 1] / factor
  332 |                     diagnostics[name + "_" + stage + "_abs_mean"] = wave.abs().mean((1, 2))
  333 |             for mode in ("clean", "baseline", "both"):
  334 |                 dc = parts["projected_flow"][:, :1] if mode in {"clean", "both"} else parts["limited_flow"][:, :1]
  335 |                 db = parts["projected_flow"][:, 1:2] if mode in {"baseline", "both"} else parts["limited_flow"][:, 1:2]
  336 |                 cp = base + gates[:, :1] * dc
  337 |                 bp = normalized - ((normalized - base) + gates[:, 1:2] * db)
  338 |                 parts["budget_bypass_" + mode] = cp + gates[:, 2:3] * (bp - cp)
  339 |             parts.update(pre_flow=base, direct=direct, baseline_hat=baseline, dual_residual=dual_residual)
  340 |             waveform_keys = ("final", "pre_flow", "direct", "raw_flow", "projected_flow", "limited_flow",
  341 |                              "gated_flow", "effective_flow", "clean_path", "baseline_path", "baseline_hat",
  342 |                              "dual_residual", "budget_bypass_clean", "budget_bypass_baseline", "budget_bypass_both")
  343 |             for key in waveform_keys:
  344 |                 parts[key] = parts[key] / factor
  345 |             parts["flow_correction"] = parts["final"] - parts["pre_flow"]
  346 |             parts["gate_maxima"] = noisy.new_tensor((core.cfm_refine_gate_max, core.cfm_baseline_gate_max,
  347 |                                                      core.cfm_consistency_blend_max))
  348 |             diagnostics["flow_correction_abs_mean"] = parts["flow_correction"].abs().mean((1, 2))
  349 |             diagnostics["flow_correction_abs_max"] = parts["flow_correction"].abs().amax((1, 2))
  350 |             diagnostics["raw_antisign_error"] = (parts["raw_flow"][:, :1] + parts["raw_flow"][:, 1:2]).abs().mean((1, 2))
  351 |             diagnostics["baseline_head_disagreement"] = (parts["baseline_hat"] - (noisy - parts["pre_flow"])).abs().mean((1, 2))
  352 |             parts["diagnostics"] = diagnostics
  353 |             if not all(torch.isfinite(value).all() for value in [v for v in parts.values() if torch.is_tensor(v)] + list(diagnostics.values())):
  354 |                 raise ValueError("Non-finite v16 stage diagnostics.")
  355 |             return parts
  356 |         finally:
  357 |             self.train(was_training)
SOURCE src/models/lstm_v16/__init__.py:360 — initialize_from_v7
  360 | def initialize_from_v7(model, checkpoint_path):
  361 |     """Load every v7 tensor strictly, allowing only a new zero-output adapter."""
  362 |     if not isinstance(model, LSTMV16Denoiser):
  363 |         raise TypeError("initialize_from_v7 requires LSTMV16Denoiser.")
  364 |     path = Path(checkpoint_path).expanduser().resolve(strict=True)
  365 |     state = torch.load(path, map_location="cpu", weights_only=True)
  366 |     container = "raw_state_dict"
  367 |     if isinstance(state, Mapping):
  368 |         for key in ("model_state_dict", "state_dict"):
  369 |             if key in state:
  370 |                 state, container = state[key], key
  371 |                 break
  372 |     if not isinstance(state, Mapping) or not state or not all(isinstance(key, str) for key in state):
  373 |         raise ValueError("Checkpoint must contain a v7 tensor state_dict.")
  374 |     current = model.state_dict()
  375 |     new_keys = {key for key in current if key.startswith("core.gate_adapter.")}
  376 |     expected = set(current) - new_keys
  377 |     missing, unexpected = expected - set(state), set(state) - expected
  378 |     if missing or unexpected:
  379 |         raise ValueError(f"Not a complete v7 checkpoint: missing={sorted(missing)}, unexpected={sorted(unexpected)}")
  380 |     for key, value in state.items():
  381 |         if not isinstance(value, torch.Tensor) or value.shape != current[key].shape or value.dtype != current[key].dtype:
  382 |             raise ValueError(f"Checkpoint tensor shape/type mismatch: {key}")
  383 |         if not torch.isfinite(value).all():
  384 |             raise ValueError(f"Non-finite checkpoint tensor: {key}")
  385 |     if not model.core.gate_adapter.is_zero_output():
  386 |         raise ValueError("A new gate adapter must have zero output before v7 initialization.")
  387 |     combined = dict(state)
  388 |     combined.update({key: current[key] for key in new_keys})
  389 |     nn.Module.load_state_dict(model, combined, strict=True)
  390 |     digest = hashlib.sha256()
  391 |     with path.open("rb") as handle:
  392 |         for chunk in iter(lambda: handle.read(1024 * 1024), b""):
  393 |             digest.update(chunk)
  394 |     return {"checkpoint_path": str(path), "checkpoint_sha256": digest.hexdigest(),
  395 |             "checkpoint_container": container, "loaded_v7_tensor_count": len(expected),
  396 |             "new_adapter_tensor_count": len(new_keys), "strict_v7_load": True}

    6 |     restored = np.asarray(restored)
    7 |     return np.sum((restored - clean) ** 2, axis=-1)

SOURCE src/utils_ecg.py:24 — prd_mecge_official
   24 | def prd_mecge_official(clean, restored, eps=1e-10):
   25 |     clean = np.asarray(clean)
   26 |     restored = np.asarray(restored)
   27 |     numerator = np.sum((restored - clean) ** 2, axis=-1)
   28 |     denominator = np.sum((restored - np.mean(clean)) ** 2, axis=-1)
   29 |     return np.sqrt(numerator / (denominator + eps)) * 100.0
SOURCE src/utils_ecg.py:52 — cosine_similarity
   52 | def cosine_similarity(clean, restored, eps=1e-10):
   53 |     clean = np.asarray(clean)
   54 |     restored = np.asarray(restored)
   55 |     numerator = np.sum(clean * restored, axis=-1)
   56 |     denominator = np.linalg.norm(clean, axis=-1) * np.linalg.norm(restored, axis=-1)
   57 |     return numerator / (denominator + eps)
SOURCE src/mecge_table1_run_v16_model.py:114 — correction_geometry
  114 | def correction_geometry(clean, pre, final):
  115 |     """Exact Euclidean error identity; the two correction channels need not be orthogonal."""
  116 |     target = np.asarray(clean, np.float64) - np.asarray(pre, np.float64)
  117 |     correction = np.asarray(final, np.float64) - np.asarray(pre, np.float64)
  118 |     target_sq = np.sum(target * target, axis=1)
  119 |     correction_sq = np.sum(correction * correction, axis=1)
  120 |     dot = np.sum(target * correction, axis=1)
  121 |     scale = np.sqrt(target_sq * correction_sq)
  122 |     alignment = np.divide(dot, scale, out=np.zeros_like(dot), where=scale > 0)
  123 |     alpha = np.clip(np.divide(dot, correction_sq, out=np.zeros_like(dot), where=correction_sq > 0), 0, 1)
  124 |     delta = correction_sq - 2 * dot
  125 |     measured = np.sum((target - correction) ** 2, axis=1) - target_sq
  126 |     oracle_ssd = np.sum((target - alpha[:, None] * correction) ** 2, axis=1)
  127 |     return {"target_residual_l2": np.sqrt(target_sq), "correction_l2": np.sqrt(correction_sq),
  128 |             "alignment_cosine": alignment, "target_correction_dot": dot,
  129 |             "ssd_delta_identity": delta, "ssd_identity_residual": measured - delta,
  130 |             "shrink_oracle_alpha": alpha, "shrink_oracle_ssd": oracle_ssd,
  131 |             "shrink_oracle_ssd_delta": oracle_ssd - np.sum((target-correction)**2, axis=1)}
SOURCE src/mecge_table1_run_v16_model.py:134 — bounded_gate_grid
  134 | def bounded_gate_grid(stage, clean, bound):
  135 |     """Validation-label diagnostic: best sampled bounded offsets, never an inference rule.
  136 | 
  137 |     Evaluate a 5^3 grid using the full two-channel Gram matrix, including its
  138 |     cross term. The current prediction is an additional feasible candidate.
  139 |     """
  140 |     array = lambda value: torch.as_tensor(value).detach().double().cpu().numpy()
  141 |     pre = array(stage["pre_flow"]).reshape(len(clean), -1)
  142 |     target = np.asarray(clean, np.float64).reshape(len(clean), -1) - pre
  143 |     limited = array(stage["limited_flow"])
  144 |     clean_delta, baseline_delta = limited[:, 0], limited[:, 1]
  145 |     logits = array(stage["base_logits"])
  146 |     maxima = array(stage["gate_maxima"]).reshape(1, 1, 3)
  147 |     axes = np.linspace(-bound, bound, GATE_GRID_POINTS)
  148 |     grid = np.stack(np.meshgrid(axes, axes, axes, indexing="ij"), -1).reshape(-1, 3)
  149 |     gates = maxima / (1 + np.exp(-np.clip(logits[:, None, :] + grid[None], -700, 700)))
  150 |     a = (1 - gates[:, :, 2]) * gates[:, :, 0]
  151 |     b = -gates[:, :, 2] * gates[:, :, 1]
  152 |     inner = lambda x, y: np.sum(x*y, axis=1)[:, None]
  153 |     ssd_values = (inner(target, target) + a*a*inner(clean_delta, clean_delta)
  154 |                   + b*b*inner(baseline_delta, baseline_delta)
  155 |                   + 2*a*b*inner(clean_delta, baseline_delta)
  156 |                   - 2*a*inner(target, clean_delta) - 2*b*inner(target, baseline_delta))
  157 |     choice = np.argmin(ssd_values, axis=1)
  158 |     grid_best = np.maximum(ssd_values[np.arange(len(clean)), choice], 0)
  159 |     current = array(stage["final"]).reshape(len(clean), -1)
  160 |     current_ssd = np.sum((np.asarray(clean, np.float64).reshape(len(clean), -1) - current)**2, axis=1)
  161 |     use_grid = grid_best < current_ssd
  162 |     selected_offsets = np.where(use_grid[:, None], grid[choice], array(stage["adapter_offsets"]))
  163 |     best = np.where(use_grid, grid_best, current_ssd)
  164 |     return {"gate_grid_best_ssd": best, "gate_grid_ssd_delta": best-current_ssd,
  165 |             **{f"gate_grid_offset_{name}": selected_offsets[:, i]
  166 |                for i, name in enumerate(("clean", "baseline", "blend"))}}

SOURCE src/v11_experiments.py:125 — build_config
  125 | def build_config(variant, seed, nv, dataset, overrides=(), epochs=None):
  126 |     from omegaconf import OmegaConf
  127 | 
  128 |     filename, changes, _ = VARIANTS[variant]
  129 |     config = OmegaConf.load(APP / "configs" / filename)
  130 |     for key, value in changes.items():
  131 |         OmegaConf.update(config, key, value, merge=False)
  132 |     if variant == "v7_max":
  133 |         config.model.loss_fn += "+max"
  134 |     if variant == "v11_no_max":
  135 |         config.model.loss_fn = "+".join(token for token in config.model.loss_fn.split("+") if token != "max")
  136 |     config = OmegaConf.merge(config, OmegaConf.from_dotlist(list(overrides)))
  137 |     if epochs is not None:
  138 |         config.training.train_epochs = epochs
  139 |     if int(config.training.train_epochs) < 1 or int(config.training.batch_size) < 1:
  140 |         raise ValueError("Training epochs and batch size must be positive")
  141 |     if config.training.get("selection_metric", "val_loss") != "val_loss":
  142 |         raise ValueError("The official runner selects val_loss; other selection metrics are not supported here")
  143 |     config.seed = seed
  144 |     config.exp_name = f"lstm_{variant}__qtdb_train_qtdb_test__nv{nv}__seed{seed}"
  145 |     config.dataset.pkl_file = str(dataset)
  146 |     return config

  106 | def build_config(variant, seed, nv, dataset, overrides=(), epochs=None):
  107 |     from omegaconf import OmegaConf
  108 |

  111 |     # declared factor, including when that same factor appears in --set.
  112 |     config = OmegaConf.merge(config, OmegaConf.from_dotlist(list(overrides)))
  113 |     for key, value in VARIANTS[variant][0].items():
  114 |         OmegaConf.update(config, key, value, merge=False)
  115 |     if epochs is not None:
  116 |         config.training.train_epochs = epochs
  117 |     if int(config.training.train_epochs) < 1 or int(config.training.batch_size) < 1:
  118 |         raise ValueError("Training epochs and batch size must be positive")
  119 |     if config.training.get("selection_metric", "val_loss") != "val_loss":
  120 |         raise ValueError("Primary selection remains val_loss; best_val_ssd is saved and reported separately")
  121 |     model = config.model
  122 |     if "max" in model.loss_fn.split("+"):
  123 |         raise ValueError("Use model.v12_tail_weight for the scheduled top-k experiment; do not also add +max")

  125 |         value = float(model[key])
  126 |         if not math.isfinite(value) or value < 0:
  127 |             raise ValueError(f"{key} must be finite and nonnegative")
  128 |     if float(model.v13_raw_cos_weight) > 0 and "cos" in model.loss_fn.split("+"):
  129 |         raise ValueError("Do not combine v13 raw cosine with the legacy centered 'cos' loss")
  130 |     for key in ("v12_tail_ramp_epochs", "v13_ramp_epochs", "mad_topk"):
  131 |         value = float(model[key])
  132 |         if not math.isfinite(value) or value < 1 or value != int(value):
  133 |             raise ValueError(f"{key} must be a positive integer")
  134 |     if not model.v12_local_gate and all(float(model[key]) == 0 for key in (
  135 |         "v12_tail_weight", "v13_extra_mse_weight", "v13_raw_cos_weight"
  136 |     )) and all(model[key] for key in (

  138 |     )):
  139 |         raise ValueError("Original v7 is excluded from this suite; retain at least one v13 change")
  140 |     config.seed = seed
  141 |     config.exp_name = f"lstm_{variant}__qtdb_train_qtdb_test__nv{nv}__seed{seed}"
  142 |     config.dataset.pkl_file = str(dataset)

12.6 本報告自動檢核
  - 五個模型各有一列合併一般結果、四列合併robustness，共25列。
  - 五個模型單NV一般與robustness各完整，共另外50列；附錄總計75列。
  - 指定結果均seed3407；指標均有限數；一般及robustness每組count一致。
  - 63組LSTM都有一般+四區的完整scope，去重後重算排名，前五與本報告一致。
  - 所有差值由CSV未四捨五入數值計算；正文顯示精度不參與排序。
  - v16兩個constant-gate jobs的本機source hash核對全數相符。
  - 本次未改任何模型、設定或既有結果；只新增這份TXT。

報告結束。
